Let's detail natural gradient smooth descent (NGC).
Function `optimize_softmax(x, n, budget, tau0, lr0, max_iter, ...)`:
Input x (float array) for a=softmax(x). To avoid softmax costs every line search? We can compute a once per eval.
Loop until deadline:
- a=softmax(x)
- c=fft_conv(a)
- score=score(c)
- if score<best: update
- peaks: p = softmax(c/tau) maybe using stable. But softmax weights all 2n-1; if tau too small, p ~ one peak. Compute gradient h = compute_grad(a,p) (without 1/tau? We'll include tau). `grad_a = (1/tau)*h` where h=2H.
- `gx = a*grad_a - a*np.dot(a,grad_a)`
- Choose step `lr`. We can use backtracking: start lr=lr_base. For attempts 0..5:
   x_new = x - lr * gx
   a_new=softmax(x_new)
   c_new=fft_conv(a_new)
   sc_new=score
   if improved (sc_new < best_score - tol): accept, break.
   else lr*=0.5
  If no improvement after several, reduce lr, increase tau (smoother) or random perturbation.
- To move, perhaps direct step on a: Instead of backtracking with multiple FFT, use smooth J value not actual score: J scaling? We can compute J_new using c_new and p? For line search on smooth J, cheaper? Need c_new anyway. Could use actual score; okay.
- However backtracking with high costs: each eval FFT; if many retries maybe slow. Could use one step with adaptive lr based on improvement history. Start lr = A / (max(abs(gx))*n?)? Use scoring.

Maybe more efficient: Use projected gradient on a with step = c0 * alpha. For non-smooth max, use active set weights p and step. Could do:
   a_new = a - step * grad_a (Euclid), then project to simplex. But projected gradient may zero many; but with small step okay. Need choose step from gradient scale. Function `make_fourier_sequence(n, amps, freqs)`:
```
i = np.arange(n)
N = n-1
z = np.ones(n)
for amp,f in zip(amps,freqs):
    z += amp * np.sin(2*f*np.pi*i/N)
a = np.maximum(z, 0.0)
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-12: return None
return a/s
```

    freqs = np.arange(1, K+1)  # use even harmonics 2f

Let's outline `mirror_descent` in detail:
```
def mirror_descent(a0, duration, tau_frac=0.5, alpha=0.1, target_eff=None, max_iters=None):
    a = normalize(a0)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
    loga -= loga.max()
    best_a=a; best_sc=fast_score_from_a(a)
    c = conv(a); cmax=c.max(); tau=tau_frac*cmax
    prev_alpha=alpha
    no_improve=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-start<duration:
       # occasionally recompute c? We already have c from previous eval.
       # compute p
       m=c.max()
       z=c/tau - m/tau? stable: z=np.empty; z = c/tau - c.max()/tau
       p=np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
       h=hankel_grad(a,p)  # 2H
       hmax=h.max()
       if hmax <= 1e-20: break
       # try steps
       accepted=False
       alphas=[alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*0.25, alpha*0.125]
       for al in alphas:
           loga_t = loga - al * (h/hmax)
           loga_t = stabilize(loga_t)
           a_t = softmax from loga
           c_t = conv(a_t); sc_t=score(c_t)
           if sc_t < best_sc - 1e-12:
               loga=loga_t; a=a_t; c=c_t; best_sc=sc_t; best_a=a.copy(); accepted=True; no_improve=0; break
           elif sc_t < current_sc - 1e-12: # improve but not best? We track best maybe; accept if current_best? Actually current_sc should equal best if best_a=a; after non-improving steps a=best? We'll keep a as best.
       if accepted:
           # maybe increase alpha slowly
           alpha = min(0.5, alpha*1.05)
       else:
           no_improve +=1
           # reduce alpha/increase tau or random perturbation
           alpha = max(0.01, alpha*0.7) if no_improve%5==0 else alpha
           if no_improve > 50:
               tau *= 2.0
               if tau > 0.5*cmax: tau=0.5*cmax; no_improve=0? 
           if no_improve > 200:
               # perturb
               loga += rng.normal(0, 0.1, n)
               stabilize; a=softmax; c=conv; if score worse maybe revert; tau*=2; no_improve=0
       # adapt tau every 20 iterations based on effective count
       if it % 20 ==0:
           lp=np.log(p); eff=np.exp(-np.sum(p*lp))
           target_eff = max(10.0, n/10.0) # 480
           if eff < target_eff/3: tau = min(tau*2, 0.5*cmax)
           elif eff > target_eff*3: tau = max(tau*0.5, 1e-5*cmax)
       it+=1
       if it % 500==0 print
    return best_a,best_sc
```
But one issue: if we always keep a=best (not accepting non-improving), then c from best; if no accepted, we don't update a. Perturb then compute new. This is gradient descent with rejections. Stabilize logs:
```
def normalize_loga(loga):
   loga = np.asarray(loga)
   mx = np.max(loga)
   loga = loga - mx
   # floor to avoid underflow
   loga = np.maximum(loga, -50.0)
   expa = np.exp(loga)
   s = expa.sum()
   if s <=0: return zeros? 
   a = expa/s
   return a, np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
```

ar = a[::-1]
W=np.fft.rfft(w,M)
G=np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(ar,M)*W,M)
# need indices n-1..2n-2
return 2.0 * G[n-1:2*n-1]
```

This restricted Fourier optimizer may find the near-optimal anti-palindromic sequence. It also allows line search easily. Let's implement top-level helper:
```
def fourier_basis(n, K):
   N=n-1
   i=np.arange(n)
   B=np.empty((K,n))
   for m in range(1,K+1):
       B[m-1]=np.sin(2.0*m*np.pi*i/float(N))
   # norms = B@B over sum = n/2 (approx); compute exactly
   norms=np.sum(B*B, axis=1)
   return B, norms
```
If K large and n=4806, B ~ 64*4806=307k floats=2.5MB, fine. We can precompute once per n.

Pseudo:
```
def fourier_mirror(n, B, norms, theta0, budget, rng, alpha=0.2, tau_frac=0.25):
    M=get_fft_len(n)
    theta=theta0.copy()
    a=theta_to_a(B,theta)
    c=conv_same(a,M)
    sc=score(c)
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc; current_sc=sc
    tau=tau_frac*c.max()
    it=0; no=0
    while time<deadline:
        cmax=c.max()
        tau=... (update)
        p=exp(c/tau - cmax/tau)
        p/=sum
        h=hankel_grad(a,p,M)
        hc = h - h.mean()
        coeff = B @ hc / norms
        delta_log = B.T @ coeff
        scale = delta_log.max() - delta_log.min()
        if scale < 1e-18:
             # try random theta perturbation
        try alphas [alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*0.25, alpha*0.125]
            theta_t = theta - alph*(coeff/scale)
            a_t=theta_to_a(B,theta_t)
            c_t=conv; sc_t=score
            if sc_t < best_sc - 1e-10: accept (update theta,a,c,best); break
        if accepted: increase alpha slowly; no=0
        else: adjust tau (increase), if no>100 random theta perturbation (theta += rng.normal(0, 0.05/K?); maybe)
        adapt tau based on effective p
        it++
    return best
```

def random_fourier_candidates(n, B, maxK, count, rng):
   best=None; best_sc=inf
   for _ in range(count):
      K=min(maxK, rng.integers(4, maxK+1))

Let's implement details.

`conv_same(a, M)`:
```
def conv_same(a, M):
    n = a.size
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, M)
    # Due to numerical negative, clip
    c = c[:2*n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c
```
If M < 2n-1? ensure.

`hankel_grad(a,w,M)`:
```
def hankel_grad(a,w,M):
    n=a.size
    ar=a[::-1]
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size < 2*n-1: pad
    else: w=w[:2*n-1]
    fac=np.fft.rfft(ar,M); fw=np.fft.rfft(w,M)
    g=np.fft.irfft(fac*fw,M)
    out = 2.0 * g[n-1:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(out,0.0,out)? Should be nonneg; roundoff negative; set 0.
    return out
```

`log_to_a(loga)`:

def log_to_a(loga):
    loga = loga - np.max(loga)
    loga = np.maximum(loga, -50.0)  # floor; may change sum
    e=np.exp(loga)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: e=np.ones_like(loga); s=e.sum()
    a=e/s
    return a
```
Floor -50 means min a=1/ n? Actually after normalization, min e^{-50}/sum ~1e-25, positive. Good.

`compute_peaks(c, cmax, tau_frac)` maybe:
```
def compute_weights(c, tau):
    m=c.max()
    # avoid tau zero
    if tau <= 0: 
       p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[m]=1; return p
    z=c/tau - m/tau
    p=np.exp(z)
    # cut tiny? p[p<1e-15]=0
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[np.argmax(c)]=1
    else: p/=s
    return p
```
Effective count:
```
def effective_count(p):
    # only positive
    pp=p[p>1e-300]
    ent=-np.sum(pp*np.log(pp))
    return float(np.exp(ent))
```

`fourier_basis`:
```
def fourier_basis(n,K):
    if n<=1: return np.zeros((0,n))
    N=n-1
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=float)
    B=np.empty((K,n),dtype=float)
    for m in range(1,K+1):
        B[m-1]=np.sin((2.0*m*np.pi*i)/float(N))
    return B
```
For n=2, N=1; sin(2πm i) zero; basis zero. But n large. If n<4, handle.

def theta_objective(params, B, n, M, max_param):
    pmax=np.max(np.abs(params)) if params.size else 0
    pen=0.0
    if pmax > max_param: pen=10.0*(pmax-max_param)
    z=B.T.dot(params) if B.size else np.zeros(n)
    # clip z to avoid overflow? log_to_a handles; but if z huge, exp underflow/overflow after subtract max okay unless inf. Ensure finite.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e100
    a=log_to_a(z)
    c=conv_same(a, M)
    sc=score_conv(a,c)
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return 1e100+pen
    return float(sc+pen)
```

`fourier_mirror` implementation in detail:
```
def fourier_mirror(n, Bfull, normsfull, theta, duration, rng, K, M, tau_frac=0.25, alpha=0.2, max_alpha=1.0, min_alpha=0.02, target_eff=None):
    B = Bfull[:K]
    norms = normsfull[:K]
    theta=theta[:K].copy()
    if theta.size==0: ...
    if np.linalg.norm(theta) < 1e-8:
        theta += 0.01 * rng.normal(0,1,K)
    a = theta_to_a(B, theta)
    c = conv_same(a,M)
    sc = score_conv(a,c)
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+duration
    it=0; no=0; alpha_cur=alpha; tau_frac_cur=tau_frac
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        cmax=float(c.max())
        if cmax <=0: break
        tau=tau_frac_cur*cmax
        p=compute_weights(c,tau)
        eff=effective_count(p)
        # adapt tau every 20: target_eff = n/8 maybe
        if it%20==0:
            target = max(16.0, n/8.0)
            if eff < target/4: tau_frac_cur = min(1.0, tau_frac_cur*1.5)
            elif eff > target*4: tau_frac_cur = max(0.02, tau_frac_cur*0.7)
        h = hankel_grad(a,p,M)
        hc = h - np.mean(h)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(hc)): break
        coeff = (B @ hc) / norms  # norms vector; ensure >0
        delta = B.T.dot(coeff)
        scale = delta.max() - delta.min()
        if scale < 1e-18:
            # random perturbation
            theta += 0.05*rng.normal(0,1,K)
            no += 1
            if no > 50: theta=best_theta.copy(); no=0
            it+=1; continue
        step_coeff = coeff / scale
        accepted=False
        for al in (alpha_cur, alpha_cur*0.5, alpha_cur*0.25, alpha_cur*0.125):
            if al < 1e-4: break
            theta_t = theta - al * step_coeff
            a_t = theta_to_a(B, theta_t)
            c_t = conv_same(a_t,M); sc_t = score_conv(a_t,c_t)
            if sc_t < best_sc - 1e-10:
                theta, a, c, sc = theta_t, a_t, c_t, sc_t
                best_theta=theta.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc_t
                accepted=True
                no=0
                break
        if accepted:
            if sc_t < best_sc: ... already
            # increase alpha if improvement significant
            if it % 20 == 0 and alpha_cur < max_alpha: alpha_cur = min(max_alpha, alpha_cur*1.1)
        else:
            no +=1
            if no > 20:
                tau_frac_cur = min(1.0, tau_frac_cur*1.5)
            if no > 60:
                # reduce alpha, perturb
                alpha_cur = max(min_alpha, alpha_cur*0.7)
                # small random:
                theta += 0.02*rng.normal(0,1,K)
                if no > 120:
                    theta = best_theta + 0.05*rng.normal(0,1,K)
                no=0
                a = theta_to_a(B, theta); c=conv_same(a,M); sc=score_conv(a,c)
        it+=1
        if it%200==0: print(...)
    # final check
    sc_best=best_sc
    return best_theta, best_a, sc_best
```

def get_fft_len(n):

    return m

Let's define `theta_objective` robust:
```
def theta_objective(params, B, n, M, max_param):
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(params)): return 1e100
    pmax=float(np.max(np.abs(params)))
    pen=0.0
    if pmax > max_param: pen = 5.0*(pmax-max_param)
    if B.size ==0:
       a=np.full(n,1.0/n)
    else:
       z=B.T.dot(params)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return 1e100+pen
       a=log_to_a(z)
    c=conv_same(a,M); sc=score_conv(a,c)

def topk_weights(c, K):
    if K >= c.size: p = np.full(c.size,1/c.size)

       idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]

    return p

def get_fft_len(n):
    if n <=1: return 1
    need = max(2*n-1, 3*n-2)
    m=1
    while m < need:
        m <<=1
    return m
```
Maybe m power of two large for n=1 ->1.

`conv_same(a, M)`:
```
def conv_same(a, M):
    n = int(a.size)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, M)
    c = c[:2*n-1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c
```
If M < 2n-1? Shouldn't. But if M=1 and n=1? 2n-1=1, ok.

`hankel_grad` as above. Need if M maybe less than needed? Use `M_eff = get_fft_len(n)`? Inside just assume.

`effective_count`:
```
def effective_count(p):
    pp = p[p > 1e-300]
    if pp.size ==0: return 1.0
    ent = -float(np.sum(pp * np.log(pp)))
    if ent < -np.log(pp.size): return 1.0
    if ent > np.log(pp.size): ent = float(np.log(pp.size))
    return float(np.exp(ent))
```
For numerical.

`compute_weights_soft`:
```
def compute_weights_soft(c, tau):
    if tau <= 0.0:
       p=np.zeros(c.size); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return p
    m=float(np.max(c))
    z = c / tau - m / tau
    p = np.exp(z)
    # zero tiny
    p[p < 1e-18] = 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
       p=np.zeros(c.size); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0
    else:
       p /= s
    return p
```
If tau extremely small, z for max 0, others -large -> underflow, p only max. Good.

`topk_weights(c, k)`:

def topk_weights(c, k):
    n=c.size
    if k <=0: return zeros
    if k >= n:
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    p=np.zeros(n)
    p[idx]=1.0/float(k)
    return p
```

Fourier basis: if K=0 return empty.
```
def fourier_basis(n,K):
    if K <=0 or n <=1:
       return np.zeros((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    N=float(n-1)
    i=np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    B=np.empty((K,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       B[m-1] = np.sin((2.0*m*np.pi*i)/N)
    return B
```
For n=2, N=1, sin(2πm i) for i=0,1: sin(0), sin(2πm)=0. basis zero. But n large.

def normalize_a(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
       a=np.ones_like(a); s=float(a.size) if a.size>0 else 1.0
       if s ==0: return np.array([1.0])
       a = a/s
    else:
       a = a/s
    return a
```

def random_fourier_search(n, Bfull, M, budget, rng):
   Kmax=Bfull.shape[0]
   best_theta=np.zeros(Kmax)
   best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
   deadline=time.monotonic()+budget
   tries=0
   # deterministic seeds
   for sig in [0.0,0.2,0.4,0.7,1.0]:
       if time>deadline break
       for restart in range(5):
          K = int(rng.integers(3, Kmax+1)) if Kmax>3 else Kmax
          theta=np.zeros(Kmax)
          if sig>0:
             sigma = sig / math.sqrt(K)
             theta[:K] = rng.normal(0, sigma, K)
          a=theta_to_a(Bfull, theta)
          c=conv_same(a,M); sc=score_conv(a,c)
          if sc<best_sc: update
          # local random coordinate hill climb for a few steps? Could add
          step=0.05 / math.sqrt(K)
          for it in range(50):
             i=int(rng.integers(0,K))
             delta=rng.choice([-1,1,0])*step
             theta_t=theta.copy(); theta_t[i]+=delta
             theta_t=np.clip(theta_t,-4,4)
             a_t=theta_to_a(Bfull, theta_t)
             c_t=conv_same(a_t,M); sc_t=score_conv(a_t,c_t)
             if sc_t < sc:
                theta=theta_t; a=a_t; c=c_t; sc=sc_t; if sc<best_sc update; step*=1.05
             else:
                step*=0.7
                if step < 0.02/math.sqrt(K): break
          tries +=1
          if tries > maybe and time budget? use time check
   return best_theta,best_a,best_sc
```
This random search may consume budget; we can set budget 45s. But local hillclimb 50*seeds*restarts may 1000 evals; okay. However, if `random_fourier_search` loops by time, we need check inside. Use `if time.monotonic() > deadline: break`.

Could be overkill. Include but limit: number of trials based on time? We'll just rely on budget parameter.

def powell_theta_search(n, Bfull, M, budget, rng, theta_init, maxK=16):
   if scipy_minimize is None: return theta_init...
   deadline=time.monotonic()+budget
   K0=min(maxK, Bfull.shape[0])
   B0=Bfull[:K0]
   best_theta=theta_init.copy(); best_sc=score from best_theta
   nstarts = min(4, max(1, int(budget/30)))
   for restart in range(nstarts):
      if time>deadline break
      if restart==0: x0=theta_init[:K0].copy()
      else:
          K=int(rng.integers(4,K0+1))
          sigma=0.5/math.sqrt(K)
          x0=np.zeros(K0); x0[:K]=rng.normal(0,sigma,K)
      res=scipy_minimize(theta_objective, x0, args=(B0,n,M,4.0), method='Powell', options={'maxiter':100, 'maxfev':250})
      if res.fun < best_sc:
          sc=theta_objective(res.x, B0,n,M,4.0)
          theta_t=np.zeros(Bfull.shape[0]); theta_t[:K0]=np.clip(res.x,-4,4)
          a=theta_to_a(Bfull, theta_t); actual=score...
          if actual<best_sc: update
   return
```

def fourier_mirror(n, Bfull, norms_full, theta0, M, duration, rng, K=24, tau_frac=0.3, alpha0=0.15):
    if duration <=0: return theta0, a0, sc0
    B=Bfull[:K]; norms=norms_full[:K]
    theta=np.zeros(B.shape[0])? if theta0 length maybe maxK; take first K.
    theta = np.asarray(theta0[:K], dtype=float).copy()
    if theta.size==K? yes.
    if np.linalg.norm(theta) < 1e-6: theta += 0.02*rng.normal(0,1,K)
    a = theta_to_a(B,theta)
    c = conv_same(a,M)
    sc=score_conv(a,c)
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_sc=sc
    alpha=alpha0; tau_frac_cur=tau_frac; no=0; it=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+duration
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # if no improve for long, reset theta=best + random
       if it >0 and it % 25 ==0:
           sigma = (0.02 + 0.03 * min(1.0, no/50.0)) / math.sqrt(K)
           theta_r = best_theta + sigma * rng.normal(0,1,K)
           theta_r = np.clip(theta_r,-5,5)
           a_r=theta_to_a(B,theta_r); c_r=conv_same(a_r,M); sc_r=score_conv(a_r,c_r)
           if sc_r < best_sc - 1e-12:
               best_theta=theta_r; best_a=a_r; best_c=c_r; best_sc=sc_r
               no=0
       # set work state best
       theta=best_theta.copy()
       a=best_a.copy()? We can use best_a directly read-only; no modification if we create new. c=best_c.
       cmax=float(c.max())
       tau=tau_frac_cur*cmax
       p=compute_weights_soft(c, tau)
       eff=effective_count(p)
       if it % 20 == 0:
          target=max(16.0, n/8.0)
          if eff < target/4: tau_frac_cur = min(1.0, tau_frac_cur*1.5)
          elif eff > target*4: tau_frac_cur = max(0.03, tau_frac_cur*0.75)
       h= hankel_grad(a,p,M)
       hc = h - np.mean(h)
       coeff = (B @ hc) / norms
       delta = B.T.dot(coeff)
       scale = delta.max() - delta.min()
       accepted=False
       if scale > 1e-18:
          stepc = coeff / scale
          for al in (alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*0.25, alpha*0.125, alpha*0.0625):
             if al < 1e-4: break
             theta_t = np.clip(theta - al * stepc, -5, 5)
             a_t = theta_to_a(B, theta_t)
             c_t = conv_same(a_t, M)
             sc_t = score_conv(a_t,c_t)
             if sc_t < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_theta=theta_t; best_a=a_t; best_c=c_t; best_sc=sc_t
                accepted=True
                no=0
                break
       if accepted:
          if it % 50 ==0 and alpha < 0.8: alpha *=1.05
       else:
          no +=1
          if no > 20:
             alpha = max(0.02, alpha*0.8)
             tau_frac_cur = min(1.0, tau_frac_cur*1.3)
       it +=1
       if it % 200 ==0: print(...)
    return best_theta,best_a,best_sc
```

def full_mirror(n, a0, M, duration, rng, alpha0=0.08, tau_frac=0.3, top_iters=200, topK0=100):
   if a0 invalid return
   loga=log_from_a(a0)
   a=normalize? log_to_a(loga)
   c=conv; sc=score
   best_loga=loga copy; best_a; best_c; best_sc
   alpha=alpha0; tau_frac_cur=tau_frac; no=0; it=0; deadline
   while time<deadline:
      # random log perturb every 25
      if it%25==0 or no>50:
          sigma=0.02 + 0.03*min(1,no/200)
          loga_r=best_loga + sigma*rng.normal(0,1,n)
          a_r=log_to_a(loga_r); c_r=conv; sc_r=score
          if sc_r < best_sc: update
      loga = best_loga
      a = best_a? We can recompute a from loga? best_a consistent. Use best_a.
      c = best_c
      cmax=c.max()
      if it < top_iters:
          k = min(c.size, max(20, topK0 + it* (500//max(1,top_iters))))
          p=topk_weights(c, k)
      else:
          tau=tau_frac_cur*cmax
          p=compute_weights_soft(c,tau)
          eff=effective_count(p); adapt every20
      h=hankel_grad(a,p,M)
      delta=h - np.mean(h)
      scale=delta.max()-delta.min()
      accepted=False
      if scale >1e-20:
         dnorm=delta/scale
         for al in (alpha, alpha*0.5, alpha*0.25, alpha*0.125, alpha*0.0625):
             if al<1e-4: break
             loga_t=loga - al*dnorm
             a_t=log_to_a(loga_t)
             c_t=conv_same(a_t,M); sc_t=score_conv(a_t,c_t)
             if sc_t < best_sc - 1e-12:
                 best_loga=loga_t; best_a=a_t; best_c=c_t; best_sc=sc_t
                 # Re-normalize loga? loga_t may have large range; log_to_a floors. Update best_loga = log_from_a(a_t) to keep finite?
                 best_loga = log_from_a(a_t)
                 accepted=True; no=0; break
      if accepted:
          if it%50==0 and alpha<0.3: alpha*=1.05
      else:
          no +=1
          if no > 30:
             alpha=max(0.01, alpha*0.8)
             if top_iters < it: tau_frac_cur=min(1.0, tau_frac_cur*1.3)
      it +=1
      if it%200==0 print
   return best_a,best_sc
```

Pseudo final for random:
```
def random_fourier_search(n, Bfull, M, budget, rng, initial_theta):
    Kmax=Bfull.shape[0]
    if Kmax==0 or budget<=0:
       # eval initial? if budget 0 return initial and score
       theta=np.zeros(Kmax); if initial_theta: theta[:initial_theta.size]=...
       a=theta_to_a(...); sc=...
       return theta,a,sc
    theta0 = np.zeros(Kmax)
    if initial_theta is not None:
        L=min(Kmax, initial_theta.size); theta0[:L]=np.clip(initial_theta[:L], -5,5)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget
    # evaluate theta0
    a0=theta_to_a; sc0=score; best...
    # deterministic amplitudes maybe skip (already in main)
    trials=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       K = int(rng.integers(3, Kmax+1)) if Kmax>=3 else Kmax
       sig = float(rng.choice([0.2,0.4,0.6,0.9,1.2]))
       sigma = sig / math.sqrt(K)
       theta = theta0.copy() if rng.random()<0.3 else np.zeros(Kmax)
       theta[:K] += rng.normal(0, sigma, K)
       theta = np.clip(theta, -4,4)
       a = theta_to_a(Bfull, theta)
       c=conv; sc=score
       # local coordinate
       step = 0.1/math.sqrt(K)
       for step_it in range(30):
           if time.monotonic()>=deadline: break
           i=int(rng.integers(0,K))
           direction = float(rng.choice([-1.0,1.0,0.5,-0.5]))
           theta_t=theta.copy()
           theta_t[i] += direction*step
           theta_t = np.clip(theta_t, -4,4)
           a_t=theta_to_a; sc_t=score
           if sc_t < sc - 1e-12:
               theta=theta_t; a=a_t; c=c_t; sc=sc_t
               step = min(0.8, step*1.1)
           else:
               step *= 0.7
               if step < 0.02/math.sqrt(K): break
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best_theta=theta.copy(); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=sc
       trials+=1
       if trials > int(max(50, budget*2)): break? Can't use budget maybe. Use time only.
    return best_theta,best_a,best_sc
```
This may run many evals. 45s okay.

def powell_theta_search(n, Bfull, M, budget, rng, theta_init):
    Kmax=Bfull.shape[0]
    if Kmax==0 or scipy_minimize is None or budget<=0: return theta_init...
    K0 = min(Kmax, 14)
    B0 = Bfull[:K0]
    theta = theta_init.copy()
    a=theta_to_a; sc=score
    best...
    deadline=time.monotonic()+budget
    starts = min(4, max(2, int(budget/25)))
    for s in range(starts):
       if time>deadline break
       if s==0:
           x0 = theta[:K0].copy()
       else:
           K=int(rng.integers(4,K0+1))
           sigma=0.6/math.sqrt(K)
           x0=np.zeros(K0); x0[:K]=rng.normal(0,sigma,K)
       res = scipy_minimize(theta_objective, x0, args=(B0,n,M,3.0), method='Powell', options={'maxiter':120, 'maxfev':300, 'xatol':1e-5, 'fatol':1e-7})
       x = np.clip(res.x, -3,3)
       theta_t=np.zeros(Kmax); theta_t[:K0]=x
       if not all finite continue
       a_t=theta_to_a(Bfull,theta_t)
       c_t=conv; sc_t=score
       if sc_t<best_sc: update
       # also try res.fun? okay.
    return best...
```

```
def add_candidate(cand_list, a):
   if a is None: return
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<0.01: return
   a=a/s
   # avoid duplicates? not necessary
   cand_list.append(a.copy())
```

Now, let's revisit the mathematical factor in hankel gradient for **weighted sum of c**. Since c_k =∑_{i+j=k} a_i a_j over ordered pairs. The derivative ∂c_k/∂a_i = a_{k-i} + a_{k-i}? As established 2 a_{k-i}. Good. Our hankel returns 2*g where g_i=∑p_k a_{k-i}. So h=2∑p a_{k-i}. Good.

Let's test with n=2 earlier. p over c. h formula correct.

Now, one subtlety: `np.convolve(a,a)` for nonnegative a computes c_k =∑_{i=max(0,k-n+1)}^{min(n-1,k)} a_i a_{k-i}. Does that match ∑_{i+j=k} all ordered pairs? Yes because for each i in that range, j=k-i. Each ordered pair (i,j) appears. Good.

Now, if `w` in hankel grad length 2n-1. Our FFT `irfft(rfft(ar)*rfft(w), M)` computes linear convolution ar*w. We need g_i =∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j} as derived. Formula slice [n-1:2n-1] of conv(ar,w). ar index r=n-1-j? Let's re-derive carefully to avoid off-by-one.
We want for each i=0..n-1: S_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j w_{i+j}, where w indices valid 0..2n-2; if i+j out of [0,2n-2] treat 0. Let w array length L=2n-1. Define ar[r]=a_{n-1-r}, r=0..n-1. Convolution (ar * w)[t] = ∑_{r=0}^{n-1} ar[r] w[t-r]. Set t = n-1+i. Then w index t-r = n-1+i-r. Let j = n-1-r => r=n-1-j. w index = n-1+i-(n-1-j)=i+j. Sum over r corresponds j=0..n-1: a_j w_{i+j}. Correct. t range i=0 -> n-1; i=n-1 -> 2n-2. Conv length n+L-1=3n-2; max index 3n-3, slice ok. Good.

Now, p weights length L. If p from c length L. Good.

```
def log_to_a(loga):
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(loga)):
        loga = np.where(np.isfinite(loga), loga, -50.0)
    mx = float(np.max(loga))
    loga = loga - mx
    loga = np.maximum(loga, -50.0)
    e = np.exp(loga)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
       return np.full(loga.size, 1.0/loga.size if loga.size>0 else 1.0)
    return e/s
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing

    score = 2*n*max(convolve(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2

using a hybrid low-cost numerical optimization pipeline:

- FFT-based exact convolution surrogate for the given evaluator.
- Low-dimensional Fourier (anti-palindromic) search to find good basins.
- Exponentiated-gradient / mirror descent on the simplex with adaptive
  peak weights (soft log-sum-exp or top-k active peaks).
- Full-space polishing with random restarts and line search.

The final answer is validated with evaluate_sequence() when it is available.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    scipy_minimize = None


def get_fft_len(n):
    """Power-of-two FFT length sufficient for both a*a and a*weights."""
    if n <= 1:
        return 1
    need = max(2 * n - 1, 3 * n - 2)
    m = 1
    while m < need:
        m <<= 1
    return m


def normalize_a(a):
    """Non-negative normalization; returns sum-1 vector or uniform fallback."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = a.copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    np.minimum(a, 1000.0, out=a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def score_conv(a, c):
    """Proxy score matching evaluator formula."""
    n = int(a.size)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def conv_same(a, M):
    """Full convolution a*a, clipped to remove tiny negative FFT noise."""
    n = int(a.size)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, M)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def hankel_grad(a, w, M):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w[k] * (a*a)[k] w.r.t. a.

    For np.convolve(a,a), derivative is 2 * sum_k w[k] * a[k-i]
    (valid k). Computed as a fast Hankel-vector multiplication.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    needed = 2 * n - 1
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size < needed:
        wp = np.zeros(needed, dtype=np.float64)
        wp[:w.size] = w
        w = wp
    elif w.size > needed:
        w = w[:needed]

    ar = a[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(ar, M)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, M)
    g = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, M)
    out = 2.0 * g[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(out, 0.0, out=out)
    return out


def a_from_log(loga):
    """Convert log-vector to positive normalized vector with numerical floor."""
    loga = np.asarray(loga, dtype=np.float64)
    if loga.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    loga = loga.copy()
    bad = ~np.isfinite(loga)
    if np.any(bad):
        loga[bad] = -50.0
    mx = float(np.max(loga))
    loga -= mx
    loga = np.maximum(loga, -50.0)
    e = np.exp(loga)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(loga.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(loga.size)
    return e / s


def prepare_loga(a):
    """Normalize and floor log(a) for stable mirror updates."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    la = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    la = la - float(np.max(la))
    la = np.maximum(la, -50.0)
    return la


def effective_count(p):
    """Effective number of weighted peaks."""
    pp = p[p > 1e-300]
    if pp.size == 0:
        return 1.0
    ent = -float(np.sum(pp * np.log(pp)))
    lo = 0.0
    hi = float(math.log(pp.size))
    if ent < lo:
        ent = lo
    elif ent > hi:
        ent = hi
    return float(np.exp(ent))


def compute_weights_soft(c, tau):
    """Softmax weights over convolution coefficients."""
    n = int(c.size)
    if tau <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = c / tau - m / tau
    p = np.exp(z)
    p[p < 1e-18] = 0.0
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p /= s
    return p


def topk_weights(c, k):
    """Uniform weights over the k largest convolution coefficients."""
    n = int(c.size)
    if k <= 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if k >= n:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return p


def fourier_basis(n, K):
    """
    Anti-palindromic sine basis:
        sin(2*m*pi*i/(n-1)), m=1..K
    """
    if K < 0:
        K = 0
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    N = float(n - 1)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    B = np.empty((K, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        B[m - 1] = np.sin((2.0 * m * np.pi * i) / N)
    return B


def theta_to_a(B, theta):
    """z = B.T @ theta, then softmax/normalize."""
    if B.size == 0:
        z = np.zeros(B.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        z = B.T.dot(theta)
    return a_from_log(z)


def theta_objective(params, B, n, M, max_param):
    """Scalar objective for theta optimization (with soft bound penalty)."""
    params = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(params)):
        return 1e100
    pf = float(np.max(np.abs(params))) if params.size else 0.0
    pen = 0.0
    if pf > max_param:
        pen = 4.0 * (pf - max_param)
    if pf > 1e6:
        return 1e100 + pen

    pp = np.clip(params, -max_param, max_param)
    if B.size == 0:
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        z = B.T.dot(pp)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
            return 1e100 + pen

    a = a_from_log(z)
    c = conv_same(a, M)
    sc = score_conv(a, c)
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return 1e100 + pen
    return float(sc + pen)


def update_best(best_a, best_sc, a, sc):
    """Utility: keep better finite candidate."""
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_sc
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return best_a, best_sc
    if float(np.sum(a)) < 0.01:
        return best_a, best_sc
    if not math.isfinite(sc):
        return best_a, best_sc
    if sc < best_sc:
        return a.copy(), float(sc)
    return best_a, best_sc


def add_candidate(list_out, a):
    """Append a normalized finite non-negative candidate."""
    if a is None:
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return
    np.maximum(a, 0.0, out=a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return
    list_out.append(a / s)


def random_fourier_search(n, Bfull, M, budget, rng, initial_theta):
    """
    Quick stochastic coordinate search in Fourier-theta space.
    Returns (theta, a, score).
    """
    Kmax = int(Bfull.shape[0])
    theta0 = np.zeros(Kmax, dtype=np.float64)
    if initial_theta is not None:
        L = min(Kmax, int(np.asarray(initial_theta).size))
        if L > 0:
            theta0[:L] = np.clip(np.asarray(initial_theta, dtype=np.float64)[:L], -5.0, 5.0)

    if Kmax == 0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return theta0, a, score_conv(a, conv_same(a, M))

    def eval_theta(th):
        z = Bfull.T.dot(np.clip(th, -5.0, 5.0))
        aa = a_from_log(z)
        cc = conv_same(aa, M)
        return aa, cc, score_conv(aa, cc)

    a0, c0, s0 = eval_theta(theta0)
    best_theta = theta0.copy()
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = s0

    if budget <= 0.0:
        return best_theta, best_a, best_sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget)
    tries = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if Kmax >= 3:
            K = int(rng.integers(3, Kmax + 1))
        else:
            K = max(1, Kmax)

        sig = float(rng.choice([0.2, 0.35, 0.55, 0.8, 1.1]))
        sigma = sig / math.sqrt(max(1, K))

        if rng.random() < 0.25 and not math.isinf(best_sc):
            theta = best_theta.copy()
        else:
            theta = np.zeros(Kmax, dtype=np.float64)
        theta[:K] += rng.normal(0.0, sigma, K)
        theta = np.clip(theta, -4.0, 4.0)

        a, c, sc = eval_theta(theta)

        step = 0.10 / math.sqrt(max(1, K))
        for _ in range(35):
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            i = int(rng.integers(0, K))
            delta = float(rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0, 0.5, -0.5])) * step
            th = theta.copy()
            th[i] += delta
            th = np.clip(th, -4.0, 4.0)
            a2, c2, sc2 = eval_theta(th)
            if sc2 < sc - 1e-12:
                theta, a, c, sc = th, a2, c2, float(sc2)
                step = min(0.80, step * 1.08)
            else:
                step *= 0.72
                if step < 0.02 / math.sqrt(max(1, K)):
                    break

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_theta, best_a, best_sc = theta.copy(), a.copy(), float(sc)

        tries += 1
        if tries > 5000:
            break

    return best_theta, best_a, best_sc


def powell_theta_search(n, Bfull, M, budget, rng, initial_theta):
    """
    Small-dimensional Powell search in Fourier-theta space, if SciPy exists.
    """
    Kmax = int(Bfull.shape[0])
    theta0 = np.zeros(Kmax, dtype=np.float64)
    if initial_theta is not None:
        L = min(Kmax, int(np.asarray(initial_theta).size))
        if L > 0:
            theta0[:L] = np.clip(np.asarray(initial_theta, dtype=np.float64)[:L], -4.0, 4.0)

    def eval_theta(th):
        z = Bfull.T.dot(np.clip(th, -4.0, 4.0))
        aa = a_from_log(z)
        cc = conv_same(aa, M)
        return aa, cc, score_conv(aa, cc)

    a0, c0, s0 = eval_theta(theta0)
    best_theta = theta0.copy()
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = s0

    if scipy_minimize is None or Kmax == 0 or budget <= 0.0:
        return best_theta, best_a, best_sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(budget)
    K0 = min(Kmax, 14)
    B0 = Bfull[:K0]
    starts = int(min(4, max(2, budget // 25)))

    for snum in range(starts):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        if snum == 0:
            x0 = theta0[:K0].copy()
        else:
            K = int(rng.integers(4, K0 + 1))
            sigma = 0.6 / math.sqrt(max(1, K))
            x0 = np.zeros(K0, dtype=np.float64)
            x0[:K] = rng.normal(0.0, sigma, K)

        try:
            res = scipy_minimize(
                theta_objective,
                x0,
                args=(B0, n, M, 3.5),
                method="Powell",
                options={"maxiter": 120, "maxfev": 300, "xatol": 1e-5, "fatol": 1e-7},
            )
            x = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        except Exception:
            x = x0

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            continue
        x = np.clip(x, -3.5, 3.5)
        th = np.zeros(Kmax, dtype=np.float64)
        th[:K0] = x
        a, c, sc = eval_theta(th)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_theta, best_a, best_sc = th.copy(), a.copy(), float(sc)

    return best_theta, best_a, best_sc


def fourier_mirror(n, Bfull, norms_full, theta0, M, duration, rng, K,
                   tau_frac=0.30, alpha0=0.18):
    """
    Exponentiated-gradient mirror descent restricted to first K Fourier modes.
    Keeps sequence in z = B.T @ theta form.
    """
    K = int(min(K, Bfull.shape[0]))
    if K <= 0 or duration <= 0.0:
        a = theta_to_a(Bfull, theta0)
        c = conv_same(a, M)
        return np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy(), a.copy(), score_conv(a, c)

    B = Bfull[:K]
    norms = norms_full[:K].astype(np.float64)
    norms[norms < 1e-12] = 1.0

    theta = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    L0 = min(K, int(np.asarray(theta0).size))
    if L0 > 0:
        theta[:L0] = np.clip(np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64)[:L0], -5.0, 5.0)

    if float(np.linalg.norm(theta)) < 1e-8:
        theta += (0.03 / math.sqrt(K)) * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        theta = np.clip(theta, -5.0, 5.0)

    z = B.T.dot(theta)
    a = a_from_log(z)
    c = conv_same(a, M)
    sc = score_conv(a, c)

    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_z = z.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_sc = sc

    alpha = float(alpha0)
    tau_frac_cur = float(tau_frac)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(duration)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # Periodic random restart/probe from current best.
        if it > 0 and it % 25 == 0:
            sigma = (0.03 + 0.05 * min(1.0, no_improve / 80.0)) / math.sqrt(max(1, K))
            tr = best_theta + sigma * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, K)
            tr = np.clip(tr, -5.0, 5.0)
            zr = B.T.dot(tr)
            ar = a_from_log(zr)
            cr = conv_same(ar, M)
            scr = score_conv(ar, cr)
            if scr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_theta, best_z, best_a, best_c, best_sc = tr, zr, ar, cr, float(scr)
                no_improve = 0

        theta = best_theta
        z = best_z
        a = best_a
        c = best_c

        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax <= 0.0:
            break
        tau = tau_frac_cur * cmax
        p = compute_weights_soft(c, tau)

        if it % 20 == 0:
            target_eff = max(16.0, n / 8.0)
            eff = effective_count(p)
            if eff < target_eff / 4.0:
                tau_frac_cur = min(1.0, tau_frac_cur * 1.5)
            elif eff > target_eff * 4.0:
                tau_frac_cur = max(0.03, tau_frac_cur * 0.75)

        h = hankel_grad(a, p, M)
        hc = h - float(np.mean(h))
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(hc)):
            break

        coeff = (B.dot(hc)) / norms
        delta = B.T.dot(coeff)
        dmin, dmax = float(np.min(delta)), float(np.max(delta))
        scale = dmax - dmin

        accepted = False
        if scale > 1e-18:
            stepc = coeff / scale
            d = delta / scale
            for al in (alpha, alpha * 0.5, alpha * 0.25, alpha * 0.125, alpha * 0.0625):
                if al < 1e-4:
                    break
                raw = theta - al * stepc
                clip_mask = (raw < -5.0) | (raw > 5.0)
                theta_t = np.clip(raw, -5.0, 5.0)
                if np.any(clip_mask):
                    z_t = B.T.dot(theta_t)
                else:
                    z_t = z - al * d
                a_t = a_from_log(z_t)
                c_t = conv_same(a_t, M)
                sc_t = score_conv(a_t, c_t)
                if sc_t < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_theta = theta_t
                    best_z = z_t
                    best_a = a_t
                    best_c = c_t
                    best_sc = float(sc_t)
                    accepted = True
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

        if accepted:
            if it % 50 == 0 and alpha < 0.85:
                alpha *= 1.05
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 25:
                alpha = max(0.02, alpha * 0.80)
                tau_frac_cur = min(1.0, tau_frac_cur * 1.35)

        it += 1
        if it % 300 == 0:
            print(f"  fourier_mirror it={it} best={best_sc:.8f} K={K} tau_frac={tau_frac_cur:.3f}", flush=True)

    return best_theta, best_a, best_sc


def full_mirror(n, a0, M, duration, rng, alpha0=0.10, tau_frac=0.28,
                top_iters=260, topK0=120):
    """
    Full-space mirror descent in log(a). Uses top-k active peaks initially, then
    soft log-sum-exp peaks. Random restarts and line search are included.
    """
    if a0 is None or duration <= 0.0:
        if a0 is None:
            a0 = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        a = normalize_a(a0)
        c = conv_same(a, M)
        return a.copy(), score_conv(a, c)

    a = normalize_a(a0)
    loga = prepare_loga(a)
    a = a_from_log(loga)
    c = conv_same(a, M)
    sc = score_conv(a, c)

    best_loga = loga.copy()
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_sc = sc

    alpha = float(alpha0)
    tau_frac_cur = float(tau_frac)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(duration)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        # Random probe in log-space.
        if it > 0 and (it % 30 == 0 or no_improve > 60):
            sigma = 0.025 + 0.035 * min(1.0, no_improve / 150.0)
            lr = best_loga + sigma * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            ar = a_from_log(lr)
            cr = conv_same(ar, M)
            scr = score_conv(ar, cr)
            if scr < best_sc - 1e-12:
                lr = lr - float(np.max(lr))
                lr = np.maximum(lr, -50.0)
                best_loga = lr
                best_a = ar
                best_c = cr
                best_sc = float(scr)
                no_improve = 0

        # Re-center/cap log-state to keep representation bounded.
        loga = best_loga - float(np.max(best_loga))
        loga = np.maximum(loga, -50.0)
        best_loga = loga
        a = best_a
        c = best_c

        cmax = float(c.max())
        if cmax <= 0.0:
            break

        if it < int(top_iters):
            k = max(20, min(c.size // 3, int(topK0 + 10 * it)))
            p = topk_weights(c, k)
        else:
            tau = tau_frac_cur * cmax
            p = compute_weights_soft(c, tau)
            if it % 20 == 0:
                target_eff = max(20.0, n / 6.0)
                eff = effective_count(p)
                if eff < target_eff / 4.0:
                    tau_frac_cur = min(1.0, tau_frac_cur * 1.4)
                elif eff > target_eff * 4.0:
                    tau_frac_cur = max(0.03, tau_frac_cur * 0.75)

        h = hankel_grad(a, p, M)
        delta = h - float(np.mean(h))
        dmin, dmax = float(np.min(delta)), float(np.max(delta))
        scale = dmax - dmin

        accepted = False
        if scale > 1e-20:
            d = delta / scale
            for al in (alpha, alpha * 0.5, alpha * 0.25, alpha * 0.125, alpha * 0.0625):
                if al < 1e-4:
                    break
                lt = loga - al * d
                lt = lt - float(np.max(lt))
                lt = np.maximum(lt, -50.0)
                at = a_from_log(lt)
                ct = conv_same(at, M)
                st = score_conv(at, ct)
                if st < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_loga = lt
                    best_a = at
                    best_c = ct
                    best_sc = float(st)
                    accepted = True
                    no_improve = 0
                    break

        if accepted:
            if it % 50 == 0 and alpha < 0.35:
                alpha *= 1.05
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 35:
                alpha = max(0.012, alpha * 0.8)
                if it >= int(top_iters):
                    tau_frac_cur = min(1.0, tau_frac_cur * 1.35)

        it += 1
        if it % 300 == 0:
            print(f"  full_mirror it={it} best={best_sc:.8f} alpha={alpha:.4f} tau_frac={tau_frac_cur:.3f}", flush=True)

    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Main entry point. Returns a list of non-negative floats.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s) if budget_s is not None else 2400.0
    if not math.isfinite(total) or total < 30.0:
        total = 2400.0
    total = max(30.0, min(total, 2600.0))

    reserve = 90.0
    deadline = t_start + total - reserve

    # Determine length and optional given sequence.
    given_a = None
    n = 4806
    seq1 = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if seq1 is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(seq1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if arr.size >= 128 and arr.size <= 20000 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
                n = int(arr.size)
                given_a = normalize_a(arr)
            else:
                n = 4806
        except Exception:
            n = 4806

    M = get_fft_len(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20240517)

    # Fourier basis for low-dimensional search.
    maxK = 24 if n <= 12000 else 16
    Bfull = fourier_basis(n, maxK)
    norms_full = np.sum(Bfull * Bfull, axis=1)
    norms_full[norms_full < 1e-12] = 1.0

    uniform = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    c_u = conv_same(uniform, M)
    sc_u = score_conv(uniform, c_u)

    best_a, best_sc = update_best(None, np.inf, uniform, sc_u)

    # Given sequence.
    if given_a is not None:
        c_g = conv_same(given_a, M)
        sc_g = score_conv(given_a, c_g)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, given_a, sc_g)

    final_candidates = []
    add_candidate(final_candidates, uniform)
    add_candidate(final_candidates, given_a)

    # Deterministic Fourier seeds.
    seeds = [
        [0.30],
        [0.35, 0.55],
        [0.35, 0.55, -0.35],
        [0.30, 0.50, -0.35, 0.25],
        [0.25, 0.45, -0.30, 0.15, -0.10],
        [0.15, 0.60, -0.40, 0.25, -0.15, 0.10],
    ]
    for s in seeds:
        if len(s) > maxK:
            s = s[:maxK]
        th = np.zeros(maxK, dtype=np.float64)
        th[:len(s)] = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64)
        a = theta_to_a(Bfull, th)
        c = conv_same(a, M)
        sc = score_conv(a, c)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, a, sc)
        add_candidate(final_candidates, a)

    best_theta = np.zeros(maxK, dtype=np.float64)

    # Quick random Fourier search.
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    rand_budget = min(45.0, max(0.0, 0.05 * total)) if time_left > 90.0 else 0.0
    if rand_budget > 0.0:
        th_r, a_r, sc_r = random_fourier_search(n, Bfull, M, rand_budget, rng, best_theta)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, a_r, sc_r)
        add_candidate(final_candidates, a_r)
        if math.isfinite(sc_r) and sc_r <= 2.0:
            best_theta = th_r.copy()
        print(f"After random Fourier: best={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Small-dimensional Powell refinement if available.
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    pow_budget = min(60.0, max(0.0, 0.05 * total)) if time_left > 180.0 else 0.0
    if pow_budget > 0.0 and scipy_minimize is not None:
        th_p, a_p, sc_p = powell_theta_search(n, Bfull, M, pow_budget, rng, best_theta)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, a_p, sc_p)
        add_candidate(final_candidates, a_p)
        if math.isfinite(sc_p):
            best_theta = th_p.copy()
        print(f"After Powell Fourier: best={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Best theta so far for Fourier mirror.
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(best_theta)):
        best_theta = np.zeros(maxK, dtype=np.float64)

    # Fourier mirror on all available modes.
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    fm_budget = min(900.0, max(0.0, 0.42 * time_left)) if time_left > 240.0 else 0.0
    if fm_budget > 0.0:
        th_f, a_f, sc_f = fourier_mirror(
            n, Bfull, norms_full, best_theta, M, fm_budget, rng,
            K=maxK, tau_frac=0.28, alpha0=0.18
        )
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, a_f, sc_f)
        add_candidate(final_candidates, a_f)
        if math.isfinite(sc_f):
            best_theta = th_f.copy()
        print(f"After Fourier mirror: best={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Full-space polishing from the best candidate.
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    full1_budget = min(900.0, max(0.0, 0.50 * time_left)) if time_left > 120.0 else 0.0
    if full1_budget > 0.0:
        a1, sc1 = full_mirror(n, best_a, M, full1_budget, rng,
                              alpha0=0.10, tau_frac=0.26, top_iters=280, topK0=120)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, a1, sc1)
        add_candidate(final_candidates, a1)
        print(f"After full polish 1: best={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Optional second full polish from the given/alternate candidate.
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if time_left > 180.0:
        alt = None
        if given_a is not None:
            c_tmp = conv_same(given_a, M)
            if score_conv(given_a, c_tmp) < 2.5:
                alt = given_a
        if alt is None and len(final_candidates) > 1:
            alt = final_candidates[-1]
        if alt is not None and np.linalg.norm(np.asarray(alt) - np.asarray(best_a)) > 1e-9:
            full2_budget = min(360.0, max(0.0, 0.5 * time_left))
            if full2_budget > 30.0:
                a2, sc2 = full_mirror(n, alt, M, full2_budget, rng,
                                      alpha0=0.07, tau_frac=0.35, top_iters=220, topK0=80)
                best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, a2, sc2)
                add_candidate(final_candidates, a2)
                print(f"After full polish 2: best={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    # Final polish if time remains.
    time_left = max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic())
    if time_left > 120.0:
        final_budget = min(360.0, time_left - 20.0)
        a3, sc3 = full_mirror(n, best_a, M, final_budget, rng,
                              alpha0=0.05, tau_frac=0.20, top_iters=0, topK0=200)
        best_a, best_sc = update_best(best_a, best_sc, a3, sc3)
        add_candidate(final_candidates, a3)
        print(f"After final full polish: best={best_sc:.8f}", flush=True)

    add_candidate(final_candidates, best_a)

    # Validate with actual evaluator where possible and return best.
    candidate_arrays = []
    for cand in final_candidates:
        cand = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if cand.size != n:
            continue
        s = float(np.sum(cand))
        if s < 0.01:
            continue
        cand = cand / s
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
            continue
        candidate_arrays.append(cand)

    if not candidate_arrays:
        candidate_arrays = [uniform]

    eval_seq = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    best_out = candidate_arrays[0]
    best_out_score = np.inf

    for cand in candidate_arrays:
        seq = [float(x) for x in cand]
        val = None
        if callable(eval_seq):
            try:
                val = float(eval_seq(seq))
            except Exception:
                val = None
        if val is None or not math.isfinite(val):
            c = conv_same(cand, M)
            val = score_conv(cand, c)
        if math.isfinite(val) and val < best_out_score:
            best_out_score = val
            best_out = cand

    print(f"Final proxy/actual score: {best_out_score:.10f} (n={n})", flush=True)

    out = [float(x) for x in np.maximum(best_out, 0.0)]
    ss = sum(out)
    if ss > 0.0 and ss < 0.01:
        out = [x * (0.02 / ss) for x in out]
    return out
```
